File Information
Collection: Lung Disease Detection — End-to-End Clinical Workflow
Collection Description
Runs the full clinical pipeline as a single ordered workflow: 1. Admin login 2. Admin creates Lab Tech + Radiologist + Doctor accounts 3. Lab Tech logs in, registers a patient, creates a case, uploads an X-ray 4. AI inference runs (mocked or real depending on backend env) 5. Radiologist logs in, reviews the AI predictions, edits annotations, sends to doctor 6. Doctor logs in, submits final diagnosis (triggers report generation) 7. Doctor downloads the PDF report 8. Admin views audit logs Use `Run > Run collection` in Postman or `newman run` to execute end-to-end. Every request stores IDs and tokens in collection variables — no manual copy/paste.
